# In pursuance of sub-Section (3) of Section 19 of the Chartered Accountants Act, 1944, the hat of Members of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India as 011 the 1st day of April 1957, is hereby published for general information

NOTIFICATION New Delhi, the 23rd November 1957 No. 10-CA(l) /56 - In pursuance of sub-Section (3) of Section 19 of the Chartered Accountants Act, 1944, the hat of Members of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India as 011 the 1st day of April 1957, is hereby published for general information —

EXPLANATORY NOTES There are two classes of members viz , Fellows and Associates They are entitled to use the letters F C A " and "A C A ' respectively after their names 'F' after a member's name denotes that he is a Fellow and 'A' after a member's name denotes that he Is an Associate The year in brackets after 'F' indicates the year of admission to Fellowship and the year in brackets after 'V indicates the year of enrolment as Associate.

The number in brackets at the end of an entry indicates Membership Number A dagger (1) before a member's name indicates that the member is not in practice A double dagger (+) before a member's name indicates that he has been enrolled under the proviso to clause (v) of sub Section (1) of Section 1 of the Act The names of such member) have been included in the topographical section only with a view to give a complete list of members Such members will, however, be governed by the conditions laid down by the Resolution of the Council under the aforesaid clause The names of Inms of Chartered Accountants, are given in italics I he names of the partners of of a him are shown in brackets after its name 'Members who are employed by a him of Chartered Accountant are described as 'with' that Inm Members who are employed by a concern other than a firm of Chartered Accouatants are decribed as 'C o' that concern The first part of the list has been arranged alphabetically and the second part of the list has been arranged topographically according to Regions The names in each region have also been ananged alphabetically (9*5 ) go6
